Correction from live dogfood: operator was attached to both owner and episode. Attachment does not imply `isSessionActive=true`; that field describes actively executing work and correctly becomes false when the episode is idle. The handoff quiescence predicate in spec-episode.ts is internally wrong: it requires `state.isSessionActive === true` while also requiring all work/action indicators idle. A successful exact-session get_state establishes addressability; quiescence must rely on streaming, compaction, bash/tools, RLM children, unfinished actions, active action, steering/follow-up queues, and queuedCount—not require active execution. Remove the false active-work requirement, add attached-idle native coverage, and keep identity/queue safety unchanged. Previous owner statement that the operator was attached only to PROJECT_CONVERSATION was incorrect.
Native verification against the live Prime Agent 0.9.5 daemon (protocol 7, get_state for exact episode activeSessionId de5a75b4cef5): activity=idle, attachedClients=1, isSessionActive=false, isStreaming=false, isCompacting=false, isBashRunning=false, isRunningTools=false, hasRunningRlmChildren=false, unfinishedActionCount=0, sessionActions queuedCount=0/steering=[]/followUps=[], taskState=needs_input, workerState=ready. Installed Prime Agent source computes status as busy only when isSessionActive or children are active, otherwise attached clients yield user and no clients yield idle. Therefore the operator correction is confirmed: `isSessionActive` is active work, not attachment/addressability. The project predicate's `state.isSessionActive === true` is the sole reason this attached-idle state is rejected. Fix must remove that clause while retaining exact identity and all independent busy/queue checks; add native attached-idle acceptance and actually-active rejection coverage.
Operator decision: canonical handoff never interrupts work in progress, regardless of invocation path. Native `/handoff`, model-callable same-session `ralph_handoff`, remote `handoff_spec_episode`, and handoff-first bootstrap all schedule canonical handoff as `followUp`, then schedule canonical execute as the next `followUp` after the handoff admission acknowledgement. When busy, current work finishes first; when idle, resume-if-idle starts handoff normally. Prime Agent 0.9.5 ActionStore provides FIFO within the same-priority `when_run_idle` lane. Remove steer wording/behavior and test current-work -> handoff -> execute ordering across every path.

OL-006 — Authoritative correction: CONVERSATION is the default top-level project-session identity
Operator correction: do not model PROJECT_CONVERSATION as a uniquely or explicitly assigned owner role. A user may open any number of independent top-level sessions in any project; each is naturally a CONVERSATION agent. Ordinary project conversation is native Prime Agent behavior. `/design`, `/spec-it-out`, and `/plan` already provide specification/design/planning. The new capability is only the ability for any such conversation to promote a spec via `/implement-spec` and reliably drive its owned EPISODE to completion.
Core product requirement: the CONVERSATION agent's episode-driving identity/instructions must be unforgettable across long ordinary discussion, context dilution, compaction, reload/resume, and episode reports. A profile/system-prompt delivery hook is only one possible mechanism. Reconsider agent construction with `prime-agent-core`, post-compaction reinjection, goal/context anchors, heartbeat refresh, or a conversation-side handoff/skill refresh. Evaluate combinations rather than preserving the rejected hook design.
Identity semantics to resolve explicitly: independent top-level project sessions are CONVERSATION by default and may coexist; ordinary forks likely retain their source personality; `/implement-spec` uses fork-like mechanics but must deliberately transition to EPISODE identity; EXPERT sessions receive their own identity; delegated children should not inherit the full CONVERSATION authority/responsibility. Identity follows creation intent and role transition, not merely CWD, ancestry, or inherited flags.
Safe failure applies to establishing/refreshing the unforgettable identity: if prime-claw cannot ensure the conversation retains episode-driving behavior, surface that clearly rather than silently continuing. Normal conversation is baseline behavior, not a special capability, though it is the source of context dilution the design must withstand.
Disposition: PRIOR DESIGN PREMISE REJECTED. Keep Slice 1 and Slice 2 paused. Revise requirements and implementation architecture before further code. The 5a5195d extension findings remain useful negative evidence, not the product contract.
OL-006 ADDENDUM — context hygiene is a hypothesis, not a CONVERSATION requirement
Operator clarification: do not infer that CONVERSATION needs an EPISODE-style externally supervised context-sweet-spot mechanism. EPISODE benefits from CONVERSATION oversight, but Prime Agent RLM is already designed for long-running tasks with auto-compaction and may be sufficient for the conversation itself. The core requirement remains unforgettable CONVERSATION episode-driving identity; targeted/preemptive compaction, self-handoff, goal/heartbeat refresh, or post-compaction reinjection are candidate mechanisms only. Start with the smallest durable identity mechanism and test it through realistic native auto-compaction, long conversation, resume/reload, and active-episode reports. Add custom context hygiene only if evidence shows native RLM loses behavioral continuity or produces unacceptable context rot.
The cited `prime-agent-core` README is filed as a design reference proving fully customized agents/system prompts are viable, not as a mandate or preferred architecture; evaluate it against lighter native resource/configuration mechanisms first.
OL-006 ADDENDUM — approved POC hypothesis, not accepted mechanics
Operator agrees with the candidate architecture and explicitly requires native proof before its lifecycle mechanics become accepted design. Hypothesis: a small universal `APPEND_SYSTEM.md` identity kernel distinguishes default CONVERSATION identity from explicit EPISODE/EXPERT/delegated-child roles; canonical role/mode packages hold detailed guidance; `/implement-spec` activates exact-session oversight mode; durable host state permits the oversight package to be restored on promotion, resume/reload, and successful compaction without an unsolicited model turn; terminal disposition clears oversight mode but not CONVERSATION identity. Prime Agent native auto-compaction remains the default; no proactive CONVERSATION context-sweet-spot workflow is assumed. Goals are not used for waiting-loop continuation; heartbeat is safety reinforcement only. Custom CLI and `prime-agent-core` agent remain possible v2 paths, not POC scope.
Required before design acceptance: native probes for base-prompt durability, non-triggering post-compaction reinjection, exact-session durable marker restoration, idempotence/failure behavior, and the identity matrix across multiple conversations, ordinary forks, implement-spec EPISODE transition, EXPERT, and delegated children. Record failures honestly and revise the spec/plan only from observed mechanics.

OWNER DECISION — EXPERT BLOCK reports require actionable remediation guidance
Dogfood showed repeated negative-review/repair/rejection cycles consuming excessive time and tokens because reports often proved defects but left the EPISODE to rediscover the repair design. Effective immediately and in the planned expert-reviewer profile/oversee skill, every BLOCK finding must include: evidence/impact; violated invariant; root cause or failing lifecycle seam; one recommended repair direction with rationale and relevant components/order but no exact patch; constraints and approaches to avoid; concrete positive/negative/failure/replay acceptance tests as applicable; regression risks; and dependencies/findings to repair together. Product decisions get bounded alternatives plus a recommendation. Reviewer self-check: can the EPISODE act without repeating the investigation? Preserve read-only independence and do not dictate code.

Idle resident sessions have activeSessionId + isSessionActive:false; `create`/current `reopen` publishes or refreshes a route and does not start a turn. Current code has three coupled defects: (1) assertIdleEpisodeState requires true instead of false; (2) handoffSpecEpisode discards a valid resident activeSessionId and reopens solely when false; (3) `{streamingBehavior:"steer",queueIfBusy:false}` is not fail-if-busy on 0.9.5—when streaming it queues a steering action. Verified safest policy: busy => send nothing and let owner heartbeat recheck; idle resident => direct idle-only prompt; no route => publish then re-read state. Require isSessionActive:false plus all detailed negative busy/action/queue checks. For TOCTOU safety first handoff prompt must omit streamingBehavior and use queueIfBusy:false so native runtime rejects if target became busy; after definite handoff admission only, enqueue sole execute followUp. Preserve uncertainty/no-replay rules. Tests must cover resident idle no-reopen, catalog-only publication, every busy cause, active steer counterexample, idle-race rejection, ordering, partial/uncertain boundaries, and native integration probe. This changes shared handoff mechanics and living spec language; no implementation until explicit operator approval.

OL-007 — official lean Slice 1 provider-absence proof is vacuous
Evidence/severity: owner review of exact pushed candidate 9d01cfc8bb40cc427895e9b862344985db81075a found every native provider probe counts only messages with role=custom and customType=prime-claw-oversee-episode-package. Prime Agent convertToLlm converts ordinary custom messages to role=user and drops customType before streamSimple sees provider context (packages/coding-agent/src/core/messages.ts convertToLlm). The original content-sentinel assertions were replaced in tests/test_conversation_oversight_native.py, tests/test_project_conversation_extension.py, and tests/test_reviewed_plan_native_discovery.py. These assertions therefore report zero even if the full package reaches the provider. Severity P1 because the approved spec and Slice 1 plan explicitly require provider-context proof of zero current full oversight packages, and the original defect was user-shaped provider injection.
Violated invariant: provider-facing evidence must detect the package after Prime Agent's actual message conversion, across active, promotion, recovery, reload/resume, queue/tool/heartbeat/agent-message, and post-compaction paths.
Root-cause seam: tests assert pre-conversion metadata at the post-conversion provider boundary.
Recommended repair: restore provider-visible content/sentinel inspection and add a positive control demonstrating an unfiltered synthetic legacy custom package becomes detectable user-shaped provider content, while the loaded conversation hook removes it. Keep exact customType assertions at the context-hook unit boundary, not as sole provider evidence.
Constraints: do not weaken to source-text/static absence, assume customType survives conversion, patch Prime Agent, or hide the superseding gate failures. Preserve lifecycle/transport isolation and compatibility resources.
Acceptance tests: positive current paths observe one managed kernel and zero package sentinels; negative control without filtering detects the synthetic package as provider-facing user content; filter replay removes it; corrupted lifecycle still blocks before provider; complete Tier 0 and Tier 1 rerun green with teardown.
Regression risks/dependencies: update all shared provider helpers together so no native suite retains the vacuous counter; preserve the exact legacy custom-message filter and the independent no-skill runtime proofs.
Disposition: ACCEPTED OWNER BLOCK for candidate 9d01cfc; await fresh Astra/max report for any additional findings before routing one bounded revision.

Operator selected revise after the required EXPERT and sole replacement both failed technically without a usable disposition; those failures are already recorded separately and no third reviewer is authorized for this exact commit.

Accepted in-scope findings for the repair generation:

1. P1 machine/process safety. Evidence: tests/test_goal_work_control_native.py starts the first and resumed standalone Prime Agent RPC processes and calls _stop_rpc only on the success path. Any assertion, RPC error, or setup failure between start and the linear stop can leave a process alive. Violated invariant: every agent-created Prime Agent process must be deterministically terminated on success, failure, and replay, and Joe's machine must never gain a concurrent second instance. Root cause seam: process ownership is not enclosed by guaranteed cleanup. Repair direction: introduce one bounded ownership/cleanup structure that guarantees termination for both processes and preserves useful failure evidence. Do not weaken the active-process guard, swallow the original assertion, use broad kill patterns, or start a live probe while the daemon is active. Acceptance evidence must cover normal completion, injected failure before the first stop, failure in the resumed path, cleanup idempotence/replay, and zero surviving owned process.

2. P1 evidence durability and provenance. Evidence: the approved plan requires sanitized machine-readable probe artifacts under docs/evidence/goal-heartbeat-work-control/, but the passing run wrote provider and hook records only beneath pytest tmp_path and retained no committed artifact. Violated invariant: a point-in-time native characterization must be independently reviewable without private transcripts or rerunning the process. Root cause seam: the harness asserts temporary captures but has no sanitized durable evidence output/manifest contract. Repair direction: define and validate a bounded machine-readable evidence artifact with exact runtime/build/commit, command, fixture/matrix rows, provider-call classifications, compatibility decisions, counts, and result provenance. Do not fabricate the deleted passing-run capture, copy private prompts/transcripts, or present expected fixtures as observed runtime output. If an actual artifact requires a maintenance-window rerun, prepare the deterministic command/schema now and report the remaining operator blocker explicitly.

3. P1 acceptance gate unresolved. Evidence: the plan requires pytest -q tests, while the candidate ran only an inventoried safe Python selection and leaves review open on whether a maintenance window is needed. Violated invariant: a slice cannot call its evidence complete while its own required gate remains an open review question. Root cause seam: the plan's generic full-suite command conflicts with the stricter machine-wide single-instance rule in an active daemon session. Repair direction: reconcile the active plan and Slice 1 evidence truthfully, inventory every native-process test excluded from the safe suite, preserve the safe suite result, and define the exact operator-controlled no-daemon maintenance gate needed for any remaining focused/full native evidence. Do not launch another Prime Agent process, silently convert skips into passes, or claim literal full-suite success.

Therefore an unsupported run missing ipython/goal/rlm-heartbeat but containing any marker/sentinel emits an error, calls ctx.abort(), and throws. Checkpoint 2 manual UAT result: FAIL at visible wait-transfer setup after the operator-controlled restart. PASS: the reloaded compatible owner session contains the V1 goal/heartbeat policy and no longer exposes model-facing pause_thread_goal or resume_thread_goal tools. FAIL: before the observable-wait test, the model created active goal 7f71dda7-9707-447a-963c-a7029eee8523 with requested-outcome-wide objective `Run visible post-restart manual UAT for goal/heartbeat work control through an observable wait and a human-only blocker, then record the result`. That written objective spans both a future external wait and a later human boundary, so it cannot truthfully be completed at the required wait handoff. The first short wait completed during the create/verify/recheck setup; its exact heartbeat 92a860aa-66c8-4016-bd3d-019602eb90c3 was deleted and absence verified, its exact marker was removed, and the terminal result was handled immediately. No second wait was started. At the resulting human recovery blocker, the owner reported the exact blocker/checkpoint and created no heartbeat; the earlier request for native /goal clear is withdrawn because forcing recovery would hide the UAT failure. No process or heartbeat remains.

Root cause: the injected policy says `bounded active-work goal` and later says to complete it before yielding, but it does not explicitly require the written goal objective itself to end at, and be true by, the next known ownership boundary. The model therefore used the whole requested outcome as the goal epoch. Repair direction: add a concise semantic clause requiring the agent to identify the next expected external-wait or human boundary before goal creation, write the goal objective only for useful work before that boundary, and keep the broader requested outcome separate. The objective must be true when ownership transfers. If an existing goal spans a boundary, preserve and report it rather than falsely completing, clearing, pausing, resuming, or reinterpreting it. Update specification/plan/canonical docs and direct policy-content tests only; do not restore native probes or broad testing. Apply/check, restart once, and rerun the same visible UAT after exact owner review. The human-blocker scenario was not separately completed because UAT stopped on this first material failure.

author: Joe Landers
created: 2026-10-12 01:02
---
Operator correction superseding both the predictive-boundary repair and the reported stale-goal blocker. Native `/goal clear` in the exact session `goal-heartbeat-work-control-episode` returned that there was no goal; trust that result. The prior claim that paused goal 62713d27-c927-4fa6-9977-16a09af8ca50 blocked execution was false or stale and requires no further goal-control action. Preserve the episode's uncommitted active-plan/spec edits only as a checkpoint, then replace the incorrect predictive-boundary direction below.

Do not require an agent to predict the next wait or human gate when creating a goal. Restore the known-good event-driven semantics formerly carried by the execute skill, generalized plugin-globally:

1. A goal owns the current active-work epoch toward the broader requested outcome. Ending that epoch is not a claim that the requested outcome is complete.
2. When the agent actually starts any long-running/background operation—examples include one or more subagents, a build/test job, download, deployment, container startup, or another process that will continue without agent action—it MUST transfer ownership even if requested work remains. Retain an inspectable handle/status location; create and verify one exact heartbeat first (the safer ordering retained from the current design); recheck terminal state; if still running, complete the current active-work goal and end the turn. Do not keep a goal active merely to avoid admitting that more work remains.
3. The heartbeat reports only meaningful state changes and never restarts work. At terminal state it deletes/verifies its own removal and creates a fresh goal only if additional agent work remains; if the operation completes all requested work, report completion without a new goal.
4. When the agent is blocked or waiting for user input, credential, permission, physical action, or product decision, complete the current active-work goal even if the requested outcome remains unfinished, report one exact resumable checkpoint, and create NO heartbeat because there is no useful machine-observable wait. After the user unblocks work, create a fresh goal before substantive action.
5. Never inject or use autonomous pause/resume. Preserve incompatible unrelated goals and narrower identity/security boundaries.

Repair the policy text, active specification/plan, canonical operational docs, and direct semantic-content tests. Remove the uncommitted requirement to identify or predict the next ownership boundary before goal creation. Keep the already-accepted plugin mechanism, capability gating, migration, and proportionate non-native test boundary unchanged. Do not restore native probes, broaden scope, apply/check, or restart in this repair generation. Produce one clean pushed candidate for owner review. After acceptance, apply/check, restart once, and rerun the visible wait-transfer plus human-blocker UAT.
